# Every catalogued galaxy in the game

Sources: OpenNGC NGC.csv (github.com/mattiaverga/OpenNGC, CC BY-SA 4.0) for every NGC and IC object typed as a galaxy, pair, triplet or group; Wikipedia "List of nearest galaxies" for Local Group distances; Wikipedia infoboxes for coordinates of galaxies not in NGC/IC; built 2026-09-23. 10828 rows.
dq: z = distance from redshift (Hubble law, H0 = 70; comoving distance for the high-redshift record holders), lit = literature distance, est = no distance in the source (the game estimates one from brightness).
This is every galaxy these catalogues hold, not every galaxy there is: the observable universe has an estimated 200 billion to 2 trillion. See RESEARCH-universe.md.
Listed in the nearest-galaxies table but not placed in the game, because no coordinates could be read from their Wikipedia pages: Tucana III, Hydrus I, Carina II, Pictor II, Tucana IV, Grus II, Tucana II, Virgo II, Eridanus IV, Horologium II, Horologium I, Leo Minor I, Aquarius III, Pegasus IV, Reticulum III, Phoenix II, Boötes V, Aquarius II, Leo VI, Pictor I, Grus I, Sextans II, Hydra II, Virgo III, Leo IV Dwarf, Leo V Dwarf, Columba I, Boötes IV, Leo II Dwarf, Pegasus III, Leo K, Leo M, Pisces V, Cassiopeia II, Andromeda XXV, Andromeda XX, Andromeda XXIII, Andromeda XXVI, Pisces III, Andromeda XV, Cassiopeia III, Lacerta I, Perseus I, Andromeda XXVIII, Andromeda XXIX, Andromeda XXIV, Andromeda XXXV, Andromeda XII, Sculptor A, Tucana B, Cassiopeia 1, ESO 294-010, UGCA 438.
